Detailed gradient algorithm:
- Work with a sum=1.
- Objective F(a)=2n * soft_max(a*a). Norm sum=1.
- Use smooth approximation: soft_max_τ(b)= m + (1/τ) log(∑ exp(τ(b_k-m)))? m=max(b) for numerical. Gradient p_k = exp(τ(b_k-m))/∑. For large τ, p approximates active distribution.

But shift by max, max difference maybe 0.001 => τ*diff=1000, overflow? exp(1000) overflow. Need cap τ maybe 500 and use stable logsumexp shift by max; max diff maybe 0.0005 =>250 safe. But as peak differences shrink, safe. Handle with stable: exp(τ*(b-m)) where argument ≤0; no overflow, min may underflow to 0, okay, sum maybe 0 if all underflow (if only max has weight 1). Fine. τ can be 1000, 10000; exp negative underflows to 0 but max weight 1. If differences extremely small, sum could be 1? Actually if b-m=0 for maxima, exp(0)=1; others underflow. p uniform over max set.
- Compute gradient p-vector via FFT convolution as above: g = 2*irfft(F_ftt(p) * F_ftt(a_rev)), length 2n-1, slice n-1:2n-1.
- Since p length 800, a_rev length 400, use next_fast_len(1200). Very fast.
- Objective soft value? We can use exact max for acceptance and update; gradient soft. Accept if exact F decreases with line search? We can do:

   We can do backtracking: try η values. Each trial: y=project_simplex(a - η*g). score exact via convolve. Accept if score < curr - eps. If none, reduce η.

Let's design final program code structure:

```
import numpy as np
import math
import time
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import linprog
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix

_LP_CACHE = {}
def _lp_indices(n):
   if n in _LP_CACHE: return ...
   rows=[]; cols=[]; idx=[]
   for k in range(2*n-1):
      js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(k,n-1)
      for j in range(js,je+1): rows.append(k); cols.append(j); idx.append(k-j)
   rows=np.array(rows,np.int32) etc
   _LP_CACHE[n]=(r,c,i)
   return

def _lp_dir(f,n,M,tl):
   r,c,i = _lp_indices(n)
   A=csr_matrix((f[i],(r,c)),shape=(2*n-1,n))
   # optional: if M tiny return None
   res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'time_limit':tl, 'presolve': True, 'dual_infeasibility_tolerance':1e-9})
   ...
```
Building A with f[i] where f is float length n; i int32. Nonzeros values all finite. Could A contain zero entries? f nonnegative but if zeros, yes. That's okay. Row nnz maybe >0. If f has zeros, some zeros in A; HiGHS handles.

But note: If f has zeros, A may have explicit zeros; fine. For n=400, 160k nnz; every row has >0.

LP time: options maybe `seconds_to_spent`? HiGHS uses `time_limit`. Good. Method 'highs'. If not available? scipy has highs. Could fallback to 'highs'.

```
def _line_search(a, g, curr_score, n, n_samples=16):

    # refine around best_t: if 0<best_t<1, local grid 0..1 with 32 points? Or use ternary? Simpler: ts2=np.linspace(max(0,best_t-0.1), min(1,best_t+0.1), 16); evaluate.
```

LP improvement loop:
```
def _lp_optimize(a, stop, seed, max_iters, lp_time=8.0):
  a=a.clip(0); a/=a.sum(); best=score
  stall=0
  it=0
  while t<stop-5:
    b=conv_full(a); M=b.max(); cur=2*n*M
    update best if cur<best
    tlim = min(lp_time, stop-time.monotonic())
    if tlim<1: break
    g = _lp_dir(a,n,M,tlim)
    if g is None: stall+=3
    else:
      # remove sign? g normalized
      # sample line, accept best if better than cur
      y,y_sc = line_search(a,g,cur,n)
      if y_sc < cur - 1e-14:
         a=y; stall=0
      else:
      stall +=1
    if stall>20: perturb a with rng normal scale 0.01; a=max(0)/sum; stall=0
    it++
    if it%50==0 and time close: break
  update best; return best_a, best_score
```
But if LP returns a g with sum >1 before normalizing, we normalize to sum1. The line search endpoint g normalized. Good.

```
def _grad_optimize(a, stop, seed, tau_start=100.0, max_iters=100000):
   a=a.copy()/sum; best...
   rng...
   eta_base=1e-3
   stall=0
   tau=tau_start
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time>stop-2: break
      b=conv_full(a); M=b.max(); cur=2nM
      update best
      # compute p and grad
      p,g = _soft_grad(a, tau)
      gn=norm(g)
      if gn<1e-14: break
      d=-g/gn
      # candidate steps logarithmic
      steps = [eta_base*mult for mult in [1e4? no]]
```

Pseudo:
```
def _soft_grad(a,b,tau,n):
  m=b.max()
  z=tau*(b-m)
  ez=np.exp(z)
  zs=ez.sum()
  if zs<1e-300:
     p=zeros; p[np.where(b==m?)] maybe m+1e-15 =1
  else p=ez/zs
  # grad
  N=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
  Pr=rfft(p,N); Ar=rfft(a[::-1],N); c=irfft(Pr*Ar,N)[:3*n-2]
  g=2*c[n-1:2*n-1]
  return p,g
```
N fixed per n, can cache next_fast_len. Good.

Potential issue: Softmax p for b values; if τ=0? start >0. If p has negative? no.

Let's consider using "bisection on M" and solving feasibility with nonconvex quadratic constraints via penalty. For a given M, want a_i≥0, sum1, b_i≤M. This is like finding a point in intersection of quadratic cones. Could use sequential quadratic programming: start with a, solve linearized constraints (LP) for a direction, step. SQP direction detailed:
```
def _sqp_direction(a,b,tl=8):
   n=len(a); m=2*n-1; M=b.max()
   A=np.zeros((m,n+1),float); A[:,n]=1.0
   for k in range(m):
      js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(k,n-1)
      if js<=je:
         A[k,js:je+1] = 2.0 * a[k-je:k-js+1]
   rhs = M - b
   c=np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=-1.0
   Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1.0
   bounds=np.empty(n+1); for i,bnd in enumerate(n)
   res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=rhs, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=[0], bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=...)
   if res.status==0:
      h=res.x[:n]; eta=res.x[n]
      if eta > 1e-12:
         hn=np.linalg.norm(h); if hn>1e-12: h=h/hn
         # also ensure h sum close 0
         h -= h.mean()
         return h, eta
   return None,0
```

Bounds h_i >= -a_i to keep a+h nonnegative (for t=1 if no clip). Upper maybe large: since eta objective, if h positive at some indices, constraints limit; if h negative lower bound -a. Upper can be 1 (relative huge). Use 1.0 to avoid unbounded.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1300,**kw):
 t0=time.monotonic(); budget=...
 deadline = t0+budget-25
 parent=get_parent(); n_parent=400
 fallback=parent_clipped; best_seq=fallback; best_score=score(fallback)
 
 # maybe quick eval structured inits, set best_init
 initial = parent
 alt_inits=[]
 for func in ...:
    arr=func(n_parent); arr=max(0); if sum>0.01 arr/=sum; sc=score(arr); if sc<best_score: best...
 # But parent likely best. Keep best_seq.
 
 # Primary optimization on 400 using best_seq as init.
 init_primary=best_seq.copy()
 stop1 = min(deadline, t0 + budget*0.62)
 res = _optimize_seq(init_primary, 400, stop1, seed, lp_time_frac=0.35, grad_frac=0.45, coord_frac=0.2)
 update_global(res)
 
 # If best > target and time left, try other lengths / starts.
 if time < deadline - 80 and best_score > 1.503:
    alt_lengths = [384,416,392,408, 512]
    time_per=...; for L in alt_lengths:
       init = interpolate(best_seq (or parent) to L)
       stop=min(deadline-80, time+alloc)
       res=_optimize_seq(... do_lp=False ? or short)
       update
 # maybe run additional gradient from perturbed best for remaining
 if time < deadline - 30 and best_score > 1.503:
    init = best_seq + normal perturbation, normalize
    stop=min(deadline-20, time + (deadline-time)*0.7)
    res=_optimize_seq(init, len(init), stop, seed+..., lp_time_frac=0, grad_frac=0.7, coord_frac=0.3)
    update
 # final coordinate on best_seq until deadline-10
 best_seq,_ = _coord_polish(best_seq, deadline-10, ...)
 update_global
 # final validation and return
```

Let's design `_grad_optimize` in detail:
```
def _grad_optimize(a0, stop, seed, tau_base=80, min_tau=20, max_tau=20000):
    a=normalize(a0)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    it=0
    tau=tau_base
    eta=1e-3  # base step unit
    stall=0
    Nfft=next_fast_len(3*n-2) # maybe local
    step_muls = np.array([1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2])
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop - 5:
        b=conv_full(a); M=b.max(); cur=2*n*M
        if cur < best_sc - 1e-14: best...
        # if tau schedule: maybe every 1000 iters tau*=1.6, reset eta? Or if stall>100.
        if it % 800 == 799:
            tau = min(max_tau, tau*1.7)
            eta = max(1e-5, eta*0.8)
        if stall > 300:
            # perturb
            a = a + rng.normal(0, max(1e-4, 0.5/n), n)
            a=np.maximum(a,0); a/=a.sum()
            tau = max(tau_base, tau*0.5)
            eta = 1e-3
            stall=0
            it=0? no
        p,g = _grad_from_b(a,b,tau,n,Nfft)
        gn=norm(g)
        if gn<1e-13:
            # perturb
        d=-g/gn
        improve=False
        # steps: around eta; include eta*0.1,0.3,1,3,10
        steps = (eta*np.array([0.05,0.1,0.3,1,3,10]))
        for s in steps:
           if s<=0: continue
           y=_proj_simplex(a + s*d)
           sc=score(y)
           if sc < cur - 1e-14:
               # accept smallest? Should choose best among steps, not first
        pick best_scand and best_step.
        if best_scand < cur - 1e-14:
           a=y_best; improve=True; stall=0
           # adapt eta: if best_step index upper, eta*=1.2; if lower eta*=0.8
        else:
           eta *= 0.7
           if eta < 1e-6:
              # perturb small, reset
           stall += 1
        it +=1
    return best,a
```

Implement `_precompute_lp(n)`:
```
def _lp_cache(n):
   if n in _CACHE: return _CACHE[n]
   m=2*n-1
   rows=[]; cols=[]; idx=[]
   js_arr=np.empty(m, np.int32); len_arr=np.empty(m, np.int32); src_start=np.empty(m, np.int32)
   for k in range(m):
      js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(k,n-1); ln=je-js+1
      js_arr[k]=js; len_arr[k]=ln; src_start[k]=k-je
      for j in range(js,je+1): rows.append(k); cols.append(j); idx.append(k-j)
   rows=np.array(rows); cols=np.array(cols); idx=np.array(idx)
   data=(rows,cols,idx,js_arr,len_arr,src_start)
   _CACHE[n]=data
   return
```
`ln` always >0. For n=1? m=1, js=0, src=0. Sequence length likely >1. But handle.

Maxsum LP:
```
def _maxsum_dir(a,n,M,tl):
   rows,cols,idx,_,_,_ = _lp_cache(n)
   A = csr_matrix((a[idx], (rows, cols)), shape=(2*n-1,n))
   c = -np.ones(n)
   b_ub = np.full(2*n-1, M)
   try:
     res=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=[(0,None)]*n,method='highs',options={'time_limit':max(0.5,tl),'presolve':True})
   except Exception: return None
   if res.status==0 and res.x is not None:
     g=res.x
     s=g.sum()
     if s>1e-12: return np.maximum(g,0)/s
   return None
```
Maybe `a[idx]` creates copy. okay.

SQP direction:
```
def _sqp_dir(a,b,n,tl):
   M=float(b.max())
   rows,cols,idx,js_arr,len_arr,src_start = _lp_cache(n)
   m=2*n-1
   A = np.zeros((m,n+1), dtype=np.float64)
   A[:,n]=1.0
   for k in range(m):
      ln=int(len_arr[k])
      if ln:
        js=int(js_arr[k]); ss=int(src_start[k])
        A[k,js:js+ln] = 2.0 * a[ss:ss+ln]
   rhs = M - b
   # clean rhs: if rhs<0 tiny, set 0?
   rhs = np.where(rhs<0, np.maximum(rhs, -1e-12), rhs) # not necessary
   c=np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=-1.0
   Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1.0
   lower=np.empty(n+1); upper=np.empty(n+1)
   lower[:n] = -a
   lower[n] = -np.inf
   upper[:n] = 1.0
   upper[n] = np.inf
   try:
      res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=rhs, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=np.zeros(1), bounds=list(zip(lower,upper)), method='highs', options={'time_limit':tl, 'presolve':True})
   except Exception: return None,0.0
   if res.status==0 and res.x is not None:
      h=res.x[:n].astype(float); eta=float(res.x[n])
      if eta > 1e-10:
         h -= h.mean()
         hn=math.sqrt(float(np.dot(h,h))) if hasattr... use np.linalg.norm
         if hn > 1e-10:
            return h/hn, eta
   return None,0.0
```
`bounds=list(zip(lower,upper))` creates 401 tuples. okay. Could use `Bounds(lower, upper)` from scipy.optimize? It accepts arrays. Use `from scipy.optimize import linprog` only. We can import Bounds? `scipy.optimize.Bounds`. But not necessary. However, list of tuples with -inf maybe accepted. Use arrays? Linprog accepts bounds as sequence of (min,max). Good.

`_lp_optimize` implementation details:
```
def _lp_optimize(a0, stop, seed, max_lp_iters=2000, sqp_after=40, max_lp_time_total= None):
    n=len(a0); a=clip_norm(a0)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score_norm(a)
    rng=...
    stall=0; iters=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop - 3:
       b=_conv_full(a); M=b.max(); cur=2*n*M
       if cur < best_sc - 1e-14: best=a; best_sc=cur
       tl = min(10.0, max(0.3, stop - 3 - time.monotonic()))
       dir_found=False
       # Choose method: first sqp_after iterations use maxsum? If maxsum fails try sqp. Or alternate based on parity.
       if iters < sqp_after or (iters % 2 == 0 and iters < sqp_after+20):
          g = _maxsum_dir(a,n,M,tl)
          if g is not None:
             y,sc = _step_search(a,g,endpoint=True,curr=cur)
             if sc < cur - 1e-13:
                a=y; best_sc=sc; best=a; stall=0; dir_found=True
          else: stall+=1
       # If not dir_found and iters >= maybe 5: try SQP
       if not dir_found:
          h,eta = _sqp_dir(a,b,n,min(10.0, max(0.3, stop-3-time.monotonic())))
          if h is not None:
             y,sc = _step_search(a,h,endpoint=False,curr=cur)
             if sc < cur - 1e-13:
                a=y; best_sc=sc; best=a; stall=0; dir_found=True
          if not dir_found: stall += 1
       if stall > 25:
          a += rng.normal(0, 0.005, n) # maybe scale 0.005? values .0025, too large. Use 0.001.
          a=np.maximum(a,0); a/=a.sum(); stall=0
       iters+=1
       if iters >= max_lp_iters: break
    return best,best_sc
```
Perturb scale: For normalized length 400, std 0.001 gives relative 40%; maybe too large. Use 0.0005 or 0.002? To escape, 0.01/n? n=400 ->0.000025 too small. Let's use `scale = max(1e-4, 0.5/n)` =0.00125. That's 50% relative, maybe okay occasional. Or 0.1/n=0.00025. Use `0.2/n=0.0005`. Good.
Maybe perturbation should be `rng.normal(0, 0.2/n, n)`. After clip, many zeros? if values ~0.0025 and std0.0005, ~some zeros fine.

`_step_search`:
```
def _step_search(a,target,endpoint,curr):
   n=len(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=curr
   if endpoint:

   # refine if best_t >0

      ts2=np.linspace(lo,hi,16)
      for t in ts2: ...
   return best_a,best_sc
```

```
tiny = 1e-12
```

Let's outline final code in detail:

```
import numpy as np, math, time
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import linprog
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix

_CACHE = {}
def _clip_norm(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<0.01:
        return np.ones(len(a) if a.size else 32)/ (len(a) if a.size else 32)
    return a/s

def _conv_full(a):
    m=len(a)
    if m<=0: return np.array([])
    if m<=512:
        return np.convolve(a,a,'full')
    N=next_fast_len(2*m-1)
    Fa=rfft(a,N)
    c=irfft(Fa*Fa,N)[:2*m-1]
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c
```
Perhaps direct for m<=512 may be slower for 400? But direct C optimized. Could use FFT for m>128 to be faster in loops? Hard. Let's use FFT for m>256. For candidate scoring in coordinate? We use maintained b. In gradient, `_conv_full` for initial/final only? Actually gradient computes b initially only; then updates b via formula. But if we perturb, recompute. LP initial b each iteration? `_lp_optimize` computes b via `_conv_full(a)` each iteration before direction. But after tangent accepted, we can update b via formula to avoid conv? - But direction maxsum uses M from b. If b drift? Recomputed after accept. Good.

```
a = a + s*d
if (a < -1e-12).any(): a=np.maximum(a,0); a/=a.sum(); b=_conv_full(a)
else:
   a[np.abs(a)<1e-15]=0
   # optionally if it % 100 == 0: b=_conv_full(a)

   # b may have tiny negative? max

```

Now length interpolation:
```
def _interp_to(init, L):
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(init)); y_old=np.linspace(0,1,L)
   out=np.interp(y_old,x_old,init)
   out=np.maximum(out,0); s=out.sum(); if s<0.01: out=np.ones(L)/L else out/=s
   return out
```

```
def _structured_inits(n, parent):
   arrs=[parent]
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   for alpha in [-1,-0.5,0.5,1]:
      f=1+alpha*(2*t-1); if min f <0, clip; if sum>0.01 add
      f=1+alpha*((2*t-1)**2 -1/3); clip...
      f=1+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*t)+0.2*np.sin(4*np.pi*t); clip...
   return list
```

Main flow refined:
```
def propose_candidate(...):
  t0=...
  budget=...
  deadline=t0+budget-20
  parent=_get_parent(); n_p=len(parent)
  fallback=parent clipped finite
  best_seq=fallback; best_score=score(fallback) using internal? maybe evaluate once.
  # quick init selection n_p
  inits=[fallback]
  t=np.linspace(0,1,n_p)
  candidates...
  best_init=fallback; for init in inits: sc=score; if sc<best_score update best? But for primary, maybe use min score.
  
  # Phase 1: LP optimize from best_init
  stop_lp = min(deadline-500, t0 + max(60, budget*0.25))
  if time<stop_lp-5:
      seq,sc=_lp_optimize(best_init, stop_lp, seed)
      if finite update global
  # Phase 2: Gradient optimize best global
  stop_grad = min(deadline-200, t0 + budget*0.65)
  if time<stop_grad-5 and best_score > 1.500:
      seq,sc=_grad_optimize(best_seq, stop_grad, seed+1)
      update
  # Phase 3: Coordinate polish
  stop_coord = min(deadline-100, t0 + budget*0.85)
  if time<stop_coord-5:
      seq,sc=_coord_polish(best_seq, stop_coord, seed+2)
      update
  # Phase 4: Alternatives / additional gradient if time
  if time < deadline-80 and best_score > 1.50274349:
      # try alternate length 384 and 416 with gradient from best_seq
      alt_lengths=[384,416]
      time_left=deadline-80-time
      for L in alt_lengths:
          alloc = min(60, time_left/len)
          stop=min(deadline-80, time+alloc)
          init=_interp_to(best_seq,L)
          seq,sc=_grad_optimize(init, stop, seed+L, maybe light)
          update
  # Phase 5: additional gradient from perturbed best if time
  if time < deadline-50 and best_score > 1.5027:
      rng=...
      init=best_seq + rng.normal(0,0.2/n,n); clipnorm
      stop=min(deadline-50, time + max(50, (deadline-50-time)*0.8))
      seq,sc=_grad_optimize(init, stop, seed+9999)
      update
  # Final coordinate until deadline-15
  stop_final=deadline-15
  if time<stop_final-5:
      seq,sc=_coord_polish(best_seq, stop_final, seed+4242)
      update
  # Final exact evaluation and return
```

Detailed code for `_grad_optimize` (maintained b):
```
def _grad_optimize(a0, stop, seed, tau0=100.0, tau_max=1e6):
    a=_clip_norm(a0)
    n=len(a); m=2*n-1
    b=_conv_full(a)
    cur=2*n*float(b.max())
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=cur
    if not math.isfinite(best_sc): ...
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    N=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
    a_rev=a[::-1]
    tau=tau0
    stall=0; it=0
    step_lim_default=1.0
    while time.monotonic() < stop - 5:
       if cur < best_sc - 1e-14: best=a.copy(); best_sc=cur
       # tau schedule
       if it > 0 and it % 1000 == 0:
          tau = min(tau_max, tau*1.4)
       # compute p
       M=float(b.max())
       if M < 1e-18: break
       z = tau*(b - M)  # <=0
       ez = np.exp(z)
       ssum = ez.sum()
       if ssum < 1e-300:
           # p uniform exact maxima
           p = np.zeros(m, float)
           mask = (b >= M - 1e-15) # maybe too strict
           if not mask.any(): p[np.argmax(b)]=1
           else p[mask]=1.0/mask.sum()
       else:
           p = ez/ssum
       # g
       P=rfft(p,N); Ar=rfft(a[::-1],N); convg=irfft(P*Ar,N)[n-1:2*n-1]
       g=2.0*convg
       d=-g
       d -= d.mean()
       dn=np.linalg.norm(d)
       if dn < 1e-13:
          # perturb
          noise=...; a=...; b=conv; cur=...
          stall+=10; tau=max(tau0,tau*0.5); continue
       d = d/dn  # unit? If we normalize d, c/q correspond to unit step; line search s is in unit steps.
       # But line search formula uses d; if normalize, c/q scale with d. Good.
       # tangent feasible
       neg = d < -1e-14
       if np.any(neg): s_lim = float(np.min(a[neg] / (-d[neg])))
       else: s_lim=1.0
       s_lim = min(1.0, max(1e-8, s_lim*0.999))
       if s_lim <= 1e-9:
          stall +=5; ... continue
       D=rfft(d,N); c_full=irfft(D*Ar,N); c=c_full[n-1:2*n-1]; q_full=irfft(D*D,N); q=q_full[:m]
       # grid
       K=32
       S=np.linspace(0.0, s_lim, K)
       Z = 2.0*S[:,None]*c[None,:] + (S*S)[:,None]*q[None,:]
       maxb = np.maximum(b[None,:] + Z, axis=1)
       sc = 2.0*n*maxb
       idx=int(np.argmin(sc))
       # refine
       s_best=S[idx]; sc_best=sc[idx]
       if 0 < idx < K-1:
          lo=max(0.0, S[idx]*0.5); hi=min(s_lim, S[idx]*1.5)
          S2=np.linspace(lo,hi,20)
          Z2=...
          maxb2=...
          sc2=...
          j2=argmin; if sc2[j2] < sc_best: ...
       if sc_best < cur - 1e-13:
          a_new = a + s_best*d
          if np.any(a_new < -1e-12):
             a_new = np.maximum(a_new,0); sn=a_new.sum(); a_new/=sn; b_new=_conv_full(a_new); sc_new=2*n*b_new.max()
          else:
             b_new = b + 2*s_best*c + s_best*s_best*q
             np.maximum(b_new,0,out=b_new)
             # normalize a_new tiny drift
             a_new = np.maximum(a_new,0)
             # perhaps if sum !=1: small correction
             sn=a_new.sum()
             if abs(sn-1)>1e-10:
                a_new/=sn; b_new/= (sn*sn) # since a_new was /? Actually a_new sum=sn from d mean not exactly0. If we rescale, b scales.
             np.maximum(a_new,0,out=a_new)
          cur=2*n*float(b_new.max())
          if cur < best_sc - 1e-14:
             best=a_new.copy(); best_sc=cur
          a, b, cur = a_new, b_new, cur
          stall=0
       else:
          # try omega? maybe use -d? If -d with sum0 may be ascent for soft objective but exact might improve? Could try quickly: use same c for -d: c_neg=-c, q same.
          # Quick negative check: s grid same, b_s = b - 2s c + s^2 q, limit for -d (nonneg). Could be too much. Skip.
          stall +=1
          if stall > 30:
             # adjust tau or perturb
             if stall % 2 == 0: tau = max(tau0, tau*0.6)
             else: tau = min(tau_max, tau*1.5)
          if stall > 150:
             noise=...
             a = np.maximum(a + rng.normal(0, 0.2/n, n),0); a/=a.sum()
             b = _conv_full(a); cur=2*n*b.max(); stall=0; tau=max(tau0,tau*0.5)
       it +=1
    return best, best_sc
```
Note: We normalize d to norm1 before feasible limit. That means `s_lim = min a_i/(-d_i)` for unit direction; good. But c and q computed for unit d. Line search steps up to s_lim. If d had natural scale from gradient, normalizing loses scale but line search explores many scales. Good. However, `d = -g` magnitude could be tiny; normalizing amplifies numerical noise. But if g exists, direction okay. If g noisy, unit direction may point arbitrary. Softmax should give meaningful.

If `d` has negative components, s_lim maybe very small if any a_i is exactly 0 and d negative. Boundary fix: if s_lim small, we can set d for those negative at zero to 0 and renormalize, then recompute c/q. Implement inside gradient to avoid fail:
```
for _ in range(3):
  neg=...
  s_lim=...
  if s_lim <= 1e-8:
      bad = (a < 1e-10) & (d < -1e-12)
      if not bad.any(): break
      d[bad]=0; d-=d.mean(); d/=norm
  else: break
```
This fixes boundary components. If still small, stall perturb.

After updating b via formula, if a_new sum not 1 (due to d mean zero within tolerance), we can correct by rescaling. But if d not exactly sum0, we subtract mean; sum ~1e-16. okay.

Line search grid K=32 may miss optimum of max of quadratics. We can use finer K=64. Cost small. Refine 20. Good.

`_lp_optimize` with maintained b and tangent:
```
def _lp_optimize(a0, stop, seed, max_iters=2000, sqp_after=50):
   a=clip_norm; b=conv; cur=score; best...
   rng...
   it=0; stall=0
   while time<stop-5 and it<max_iters:
      if cur<best... 
      M=b.max()
      tl = min(8.0, max(0.3, stop-5-time))
      improved=False
      # method choice: use maxsum for it<sqp_after, else sqp; if fail try other
      order = [0,1] if it < sqp_after else [1,0]
      for typ in order:
         dir=None
         if typ==0:
            g=_maxsum_dir(a,n,M,tl)
            if g is not None:
               d=g-a; d-=d.mean()
               if norm>1e-12:
                  a2,b2,sc2,ok=_tangent_line_search(a,b,d,cur,n)
                  if ok and sc2<cur-1e-13: accept; improved=True; break
         else:
            h,eta=_sqp_dir(a,b,n,tl)
            if h is not None:
               d=h; d-=d.mean(); d/=norm
               a2,...=_tangent_line_search...
               if ok: accept
      if not improved:
         stall+=1
         if stall>20:
            perturb; b=conv; cur=score; stall=0
      it+=1
   return best,best_sc
```
`_tangent_line_search` should normalize d to unit? It can take direction and normalize inside to scale independent. Do that:
```
d = d - d.mean(); d/=norm; boundary fix; s_lim...
```
Then line search s in unit scale.

If `_maxsum_dir` returns g, d sum0 by normalization of both? g normalized sum1, a sum1; d sum0. Good.

`_sqp_dir` returns h already normalized; d sum0.

`_tangent_line_search` fallback if s_lim tiny:

```
for s: y=a+s*d; y=clip; sn=sum; if sn>0 y/=sn; b_new=conv; sc... if improve accept return
```
Since limited steps. If none, no.

Now `_coord_polish` implementation with vectorized grid and prefix/suffix. Need use `_conv_full` for recompute at start and periodically.
```
def _coord_polish(a0, stop, max_sweeps=300, seed=123):
   a=clip_norm(a0); n=len(a); m=2*n-1
   b=_conv_full(a); cur=2*n*b.max(); best=a.copy(); best_sc=cur
   two_a=2*a
   pref,suff=_psmax(b)
   rng=...
   for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
      if time>stop-5: break
      improved=False
      order=rng.permutation(n)
      for i in order:
         if time>stop-5: break
         old=a[i]
         # if old==0? still try
         x_high = min(0.5, max(5.0/n, 10.0*old, 5.0*a.max()))
         if x_high < old: x_high=old*1.5+1e-3
         K=48
         xs=np.linspace(0, x_high, K)
         d=xs-old
         # avoid d causing S_new<=0.01? Since S=1, old<1, if old close1 and x=0 S=1-old; but a values small. check S_new = 1+d > 1e-6
         valid = (1+d) > 1e-6
         if not any valid continue
         aff = b[i:i+n]  # length n
         # out_max
         left = i-1; right = i+n
         om = -np.inf
         if left>=0: om=max(om, pref[left])
         if right<m: om=max(om, suff[right])
         # use aff and two_a
         mat = aff[None,:] + d[None?]*? Actually d length K
         Z = d[:,None]*two_a[None,:]
         if i < n: Z[:,i] += d*d
         new_aff = aff[None,:] + Z
         max_aff = new_aff.max(axis=1)
         maxb = np.maximum(max_aff, om)
         Snew=1+d
         sc=2*n*maxb/(Snew*Snew)
         # only valid
         if not valid.all(): sc[~valid]=inf
         idx=argmin(sc)
         if not finite skip
         best_x=xs[idx]; best_scand=sc[idx]
         # refine if 0<idx<K-1 or endpoint?
         if best_scand < cur - 1e-13:
            # fine grid around best_x
            dw = x_high/(K-1)
            lo=max(0,best_x-3*dw); hi=min(x_high,best_x+3*dw)
            xf=np.linspace(lo,hi,24)
            df=xf-old; Snewf=1+df; validf=(Snewf>1e-6)
            Zf = df[:,None]*two_a[None,:]; Zf[:,i]+=df*df
            max_afff=(aff+Zf).max(axis=1)
            maxbf=np.maximum(max_afff, om)
            scf=2*n*maxbf/(Snewf*Snewf); scf[~validf]=inf
            j=argmin(scf); if scf[j]<best_scand: best_x=xf[j]; best_scand=scf[j]
            # accept if < cur
            if best_scand < cur - 1e-13:
                delta = best_x-old
                b[i:i+n] += delta*two_a
                # note if delta=0? no
                b[2*i] += delta*delta
                # a update and normalization
                a[i]=best_x
                Snew=1+delta
                if abs(Snew-1)>1e-12:
                   a /= Snew
                   b /= (Snew*Snew)
                   two_a = 2*a
                np.maximum(a,0,out=a)
                np.maximum(b,0,out=b)
                cur = 2*n*float(b.max())
                pref,suff=_psmax(b)
                if cur < best_sc - 1e-13:
                   best_sc=cur; best=a.copy()
                improved=True
      if not improved:
         # compute exact b to ensure; if no improve break
         b=_conv_full(a); cur=2*n*b.max(); two_a=2*a; pref,suff=_psmax(b)
         if cur < best_sc -1e-13: best...
         break
   return best,best_sc
```
Need update `two_a` if old? We use two_a before modifying; good. If renormalize, two_a changed.
Prefix/suffix update after each accept O(m). Could rebuild only after accept; okay. If many accepts, 400*800=320k ops per sweep, fine.

```
def _c_q_for_d(a,d):
   n=len(a); m=2*n-1
   if n <= 128:
      q=np.convolve(d,d,'full')[:m]
      c=np.convolve(d,a[::-1],'full')[n-1:2*n-1]
      return c,q
   N=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
   D=rfft(d,N); Ar=rfft(a[::-1],N)
   c_full=irfft(D*Ar,N)[:3*n-2]
   c=c_full[n-1:2*n-1]
   q_full=irfft(D*D,N)[:m]
   return c,q
```

```
def _tangent_line_search(a,b,d,curr,n):
    d=np.asarray(d,float); d=d-d.mean()
    dn=math.sqrt(float(np.dot(d,d)))? use np.linalg.norm
    if dn < 1e-14: return a,b,curr,False
    d=d/dn
    for _ in range(3):
       neg=d < -1e-14
       if np.any(neg): s_lim=float(np.min(a[neg]/(-d[neg])))
       else: s_lim=1.0
       if s_lim <= 1e-8:
          bad=(a < 1e-10) & (d < 0)
          if not bad.any(): break
          d[bad]=0.0
          d -= d.mean(); dn=np.linalg.norm(d)
          if dn<1e-14: return ...
          d=d/dn
       else: break
    s_lim = min(1.0, max(1e-8, s_lim*0.999))
    if s_lim <= 1e-9:
       # fallback clip search
       return _clip_line_search(a,b,d,curr,n)
    c,q=_c_q_for_d(a,d)
    K=48
    S=np.linspace(0,s_lim,K)
    Z=2*S[:,None]*c + (S*S)[:,None]*q
    maxb=np.maximum(b + Z[None?], axis=1)
```

```
def _clip_line_search(a,b,d,curr,n):
    steps=[1e-5,3e-5,1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,0.03]
    best_a=a; best_b=b; best_sc=curr
    for s in steps:
       y=a+s*d
       y=np.maximum(y,0); sn=y.sum()
       if sn<1e-10: continue
       y/=sn
       b_new=_conv_full(y)
       sc=2*n*b_new.max()
       if sc<best_sc-1e-14: best...
    if best_sc<curr-1e-13: return best_a,best_b,best_sc,True
    return a,b,curr,False
```

```
def _psmax(b):
   m=len(b); pref=np.empty(m); cur=-np.inf
   for i in range(m):
      if b[i]>cur: cur=b[i]
      pref[i]=cur
   suff=np.empty(m); cur=-np.inf
   for i in range(m-1,-1,-1):
      if b[i]>cur: cur=b[i]
      suff[i]=cur
   return pref,suff
```

```
def _perturb(a, seed_rng, scale):
   n=len(a); a=a+rng.normal(0, scale, n); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-8: a=np.ones(n)/n else a/=s; return a
```

```python
import math
import time

import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import linprog
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix

_TARGET = 1.50274349
_CACHE = {}


def _get_parent():
    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    try:
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size and float(p.sum()) >= 0.01:
                return p
    except Exception:
        pass
    return np.ones(400, dtype=np.float64)


def _clip_norm(a, n=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if n is not None:
        if a.size < n:
            a = np.concatenate((a, np.zeros(n - a.size, dtype=np.float64)))
        else:
            a = a[:n]
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-8:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64) / len(a)
    return a / s


def _conv_full(a):
    m = len(a)
    if m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if m <= 64:
        return np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    N = next_fast_len(2 * m - 1)
    F = rfft(a, N)
    c = irfft(F * F, N)[: 2 * m - 1]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _score_unnorm(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
        return math.inf
    b = _conv_full(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    v = 2.0 * len(a) * float(b.max()) / (s * s)
    return float(v) if math.isfinite(v) else math.inf


def _score_norm(b):
    if b is None:
        return math.inf
    if b.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    m = float(b.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return math.inf
    return 2.0 * len(b) * m


def _psmax(b):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    pref = np.maximum.accumulate(b)
    suff = np.maximum.accumulate(b[::-1])[::-1]
    return pref, suff


def _lp_cache(n):
    if n in _CACHE:
        return _CACHE[n]
    m = 2 * n - 1
    rows = []
    cols = []
    idx = []
    js_arr = np.empty(m, dtype=np.int32)
    ln_arr = np.empty(m, dtype=np.int32)
    ss_arr = np.empty(m, dtype=np.int32)

    for k in range(m):
        js = max(0, k - n + 1)
        je = min(k, n - 1)
        ln = je - js + 1
        js_arr[k] = js
        ln_arr[k] = ln
        ss_arr[k] = k - je
        for j in range(js, je + 1):
            rows.append(k)
            cols.append(j)
            idx.append(k - j)

    data = (
        np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int32),
        np.asarray(cols, dtype=np.int32),
        np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.int32),
        js_arr,
        ln_arr,
        ss_arr,
    )
    _CACHE[n] = data
    return data


def _maxsum_dir(a, M, tl):
    n = len(a)
    rows, cols, idx, _, _, _ = _lp_cache(n)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    try:
        A = csr_matrix((np.asarray(a[idx], dtype=np.float64), (rows, cols)), shape=(m, n))
        c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b_ub = np.full(m, float(M), dtype=np.float64)
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=[(0.0, None)] * n,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": max(0.3, float(tl)), "presolve": True},
        )
        if res.status == 0 and res.x is not None:
            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
            s = float(g.sum())
            if s > 1e-12:
                return g / s
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def _sqp_dir(a, b, M, tl):
    n = len(a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    try:
        _, _, _, js_arr, ln_arr, ss_arr = _lp_cache(n)
        A = np.zeros((m, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A[:, n] = 1.0
        for k in range(m):
            ln = int(ln_arr[k])
            if ln > 0:
                js = int(js_arr[k])
                ss = int(ss_arr[k])
                A[k, js:js + ln] = 2.0 * a[ss:ss + ln]

        rhs = np.asarray(M - b, dtype=np.float64)
        rhs[rhs < 0.0] = 0.0

        c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        c[n] = -1.0

        Aeq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        Aeq[0, :n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)

        lower = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        upper = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        lower[:n] = -a
        upper[:n] = 1.0
        lower[n] = -np.inf
        upper[n] = np.inf

        bounds = list(zip(lower.tolist(), upper.tolist()))

        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=rhs,
            A_eq=Aeq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": max(0.3, float(tl)), "presolve": True},
        )
        if res.status == 0 and res.x is not None:
            h = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
            eta = float(res.x[n])
            if eta > 1e-10:
                h -= h.mean()
                hn = float(np.linalg.norm(h))
                if hn > 1e-10:
                    return h / hn, eta
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None, 0.0


def _c_q_for_d(a, d):
    n = len(a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 96:
        c = np.convolve(d, a, mode="full")[:m]
        q = np.convolve(d, d, mode="full")[:m]
        return c, q
    N = next_fast_len(m)
    D = rfft(d, N)
    A = rfft(a, N)
    c = irfft(D * A, N)[:m]
    q = irfft(D * D, N)[:m]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
    return c, q


def _clip_line_search(a, b, d, curr, n):
    steps = (1e-5, 3e-5, 1e-4, 3e-4, 1e-3, 3e-3, 1e-2, 3e-2, 8e-2)
    best_a, best_b, best_sc = a, b, curr
    for s in steps:
        y = a + s * d
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
        sy = float(y.sum())
        if sy <= 1e-10:
            continue
        y /= sy
        by = _conv_full(y)
        sc = 2.0 * n * float(by.max())
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_a, best_b, best_sc = y, by, sc
    if best_sc < curr - 1e-13:
        return best_a, best_b, best_sc, True
    return a, b, curr, False


def _tangent_line_search(a, b, d, curr, n):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)
    if d.size != n:
        return a, b, curr, False
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
        return a, b, curr, False

    d = d - d.mean()
    dn = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
    if dn < 1e-14:
        return a, b, curr, False
    d /= dn

    for _ in range(3):
        neg = d < -1e-14
        if np.any(neg):
            s_lim = float(np.min(a[neg] / (-d[neg])))
        else:
            s_lim = 1.0
        s_lim = min(1.0, max(s_lim, 0.0))
        if s_lim > 1e-8:
            break
        bad = (a < 1e-10) & (d < 0.0)
        if not np.any(bad):
            break
        d[bad] = 0.0
        d -= d.mean()
        dn = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if dn < 1e-14:
            return a, b, curr, False
        d /= dn

    s_lim = min(1.0, max(1e-9, s_lim * 0.999))
    if s_lim <= 1e-9:
        return _clip_line_search(a, b, d, curr, n)

    c, q = _c_q_for_d(a, d)
    K = 56
    S = np.linspace(0.0, s_lim, K)
    Z = 2.0 * S[:, None] * c[None, :] + (S * S)[:, None] * q[None, :]
    vals = 2.0 * n * np.maximum(b[None, :] + Z, axis=1)
    vals[~np.isfinite(vals)] = np.inf

    idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
    s_best = float(S[idx])
    sc_best = float(vals[idx])

    if 0 < idx < K - 1 and math.isfinite(sc_best):
        lo = max(0.0, S[idx] * 0.45)
        hi = min(s_lim, S[idx] * 1.55)
        S2 = np.linspace(lo, hi, 24)
        Z2 = 2.0 * S2[:, None] * c[None, :] + (S2 * S2)[:, None] * q[None, :]
        vals2 = 2.0 * n * np.maximum(b[None, :] + Z2, axis=1)
        vals2[~np.isfinite(vals2)] = np.inf
        j = int(np.argmin(vals2))
        if vals2[j] < sc_best - 1e-15:
            s_best = float(S2[j])
            sc_best = float(vals2[j])

    if not math.isfinite(sc_best) or sc_best >= curr - 1e-13:
        return a, b, curr, False

    a_new = a + s_best * d
    if np.any(a_new < -1e-10):
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
        sn = float(a_new.sum())
        if sn <= 1e-10:
            return a, b, curr, False
        a_new /= sn
        b_new = _conv_full(a_new)
        sc_new = 2.0 * n * float(b_new.max())
    else:
        b_new = b + 2.0 * s_best * c + (s_best * s_best) * q
        np.maximum(b_new, 0.0, out=b_new)
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
        sn = float(a_new.sum())
        if sn <= 1e-10:
            return a, b, curr, False
        if abs(sn - 1.0) > 1e-9:
            a_new /= sn
            b_new /= (sn * sn)
        sc_new = 2.0 * n * float(b_new.max())

    if math.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < curr - 1e-13:
        return a_new, b_new, float(sc_new), True
    return a, b, curr, False


def _perturb(a, rng, scale=None):
    n = len(a)
    if scale is None:
        scale = max(1e-5, 0.25 / max(1, n))
    y = a + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if s <= 1e-10:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return y / s


def _lp_optimize(a0, stop, seed):
    a = _clip_norm(a0)
    n = len(a)
    b = _conv_full(a)
    cur = 2.0 * n * float(b.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = cur

    if not math.isfinite(best_sc):
        return best_a, best_sc

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    it = 0
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop - 6.0:
        if cur < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_sc = cur
            best_a = a.copy()

        M = float(b.max())
        if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        remaining = stop - 6.0 - time.monotonic()
        if remaining <= 0.3:
            break
        tl = min(9.0, max(0.3, remaining * 0.7))

        improved = False

        order = (0, 1) if it < 45 else (1, 0)
        for typ in order:
            if time.monotonic() > stop - 6.0:
                break
            if typ == 0:
                g = _maxsum_dir(a, M, tl)
                if g is None:
                    continue
                d = g - a
                d -= d.mean()
                if float(np.linalg.norm(d)) < 1e-12:
                    continue
                a2, b2, sc2, ok = _tangent_line_search(a, b, d, cur, n)
                if ok and sc2 < cur - 1e-13:
                    a, b, cur = a2, b2, sc2
                    if cur < best_sc - 1e-14:
                        best_sc = cur
                        best_a = a.copy()
                    stall = 0
                    improved = True
                    break
            else:
                h, eta = _sqp_dir(a, b, M, tl)
                if h is None:
                    continue
                d = h
                d -= d.mean()
                if float(np.linalg.norm(d)) < 1e-12:
                    continue
                a2, b2, sc2, ok = _tangent_line_search(a, b, d, cur, n)
                if ok and sc2 < cur - 1e-13:
                    a, b, cur = a2, b2, sc2
                    if cur < best_sc - 1e-14:
                        best_sc = cur
                        best_a = a.copy()
                    stall = 0
                    improved = True
                    break

        if not improved:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 25:
                a = _perturb(a, rng, 0.25 / max(1, n))
                b = _conv_full(a)
                cur = 2.0 * n * float(b.max())
                if cur < best_sc - 1e-14:
                    best_sc = cur
                    best_a = a.copy()
                stall = 0

        it += 1
        if it > 2500:
            break

    if cur < best_sc - 1e-14:
        best_sc = cur
        best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a, best_sc


def _grad_optimize(a0, stop, seed):
    a = _clip_norm(a0)
    n = len(a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    b = _conv_full(a)
    cur = 2.0 * n * float(b.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = cur

    if not math.isfinite(best_sc):
        return best_a, best_sc

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    N = next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)
    tau = 80.0
    tau_max = 8.0e5
    it = 0
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop - 6.0:
        if cur < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_sc = cur
            best_a = a.copy()

        if it % 900 == 899 and tau < tau_max:
            tau = min(tau_max, tau * 1.45)

        M = float(b.max())
        if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        z = tau * (b - M)
        ez = np.exp(z)
        ssum = float(ez.sum())
        if ssum <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(ssum):
            p = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            mask = b >= M
            if not np.any(mask):
                p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
            else:
                p[mask] = 1.0 / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        else:
            p = ez / ssum

        P = rfft(p, N)
        Ar = rfft(a[::-1], N)
        convg = irfft(P * Ar, N)[n - 1: 2 * n - 1]
        g = 2.0 * np.asarray(convg, dtype=np.float64)

        d = -g
        d -= d.mean()
        dn = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if dn < 1e-13:
            a = _perturb(a, rng, max(1e-5, 0.25 / n))
            b = _conv_full(a)
            cur = 2.0 * n * float(b.max())
            stall += 10
            tau = max(80.0, tau * 0.5)
            continue
        d /= dn

        for _ in range(3):
            neg = d < -1e-14
            if np.any(neg):
                s_lim = float(np.min(a[neg] / (-d[neg])))
            else:
                s_lim = 1.0
            s_lim = min(1.0, max(s_lim, 0.0))
            if s_lim > 1e-8:
                break
            bad = (a < 1e-10) & (d < 0.0)
            if not np.any(bad):
                break
            d[bad] = 0.0
            d -= d.mean()
            dn = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
            if dn < 1e-14:
                break
            d /= dn
        else:
            s_lim = 0.0

        s_lim = min(1.0, max(1e-9, s_lim * 0.999))
        if s_lim <= 1e-9:
            a = _perturb(a, rng, max(1e-5, 0.2 / n))
            b = _conv_full(a)
            cur = 2.0 * n * float(b.max())
            stall += 5
            tau = max(80.0, tau * 0.6)
            it += 1
            continue

        D = rfft(d, N)
        A = rfft(a, N)
        c = np.asarray(irfft(D * A, N)[:m], dtype=np.float64)
        q = np.asarray(irfft(D * D, N)[:m], dtype=np.float64)

        K = 64
        S = np.linspace(0.0, s_lim, K)
        Z = 2.0 * S[:, None] * c[None, :] + (S * S)[:, None] * q[None, :]
        vals = 2.0 * n * np.maximum(b[None, :] + Z, axis=1)
        vals[~np.isfinite(vals)] = np.inf

        idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
        s_best = float(S[idx])
        sc_best = float(vals[idx])

        if 0 < idx < K - 1 and math.isfinite(sc_best):
            lo = max(0.0, S[idx] * 0.45)
            hi = min(s_lim, S[idx] * 1.55)
            S2 = np.linspace(lo, hi, 28)
            Z2 = 2.0 * S2[:, None] * c[None, :] + (S2 * S2)[:, None] * q[None, :]
            vals2 = 2.0 * n * np.maximum(b[None, :] + Z2, axis=1)
            vals2[~np.isfinite(vals2)] = np.inf
            j = int(np.argmin(vals2))
            if vals2[j] < sc_best - 1e-15:
                s_best = float(S2[j])
                sc_best = float(vals2[j])

        if math.isfinite(sc_best) and sc_best < cur - 1e-13:
            a_new = a + s_best * d
            if np.any(a_new < -1e-10):
                a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
                sn = float(a_new.sum())
                if sn <= 1e-10:
                    a_new = _perturb(a, rng, max(1e-5, 0.2 / n))
                    b = _conv_full(a)
                    cur = 2.0 * n * float(b.max())
                    stall += 5
                    it += 1
                    continue
                a_new /= sn
                b_new = _conv_full(a_new)
                sc_new = 2.0 * n * float(b_new.max())
            else:
                b_new = b + 2.0 * s_best * c + (s_best * s_best) * q
                np.maximum(b_new, 0.0, out=b_new)
                a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
                sn = float(a_new.sum())
                if sn <= 1e-10:
                    a_new = _perturb(a, rng, max(1e-5, 0.2 / n))
                    b = _conv_full(a)
                    cur = 2.0 * n * float(b.max())
                    stall += 5
                    it += 1
                    continue
                if abs(sn - 1.0) > 1e-9:
                    a_new /= sn
                    b_new /= (sn * sn)
                sc_new = 2.0 * n * float(b_new.max())

            if math.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < cur - 1e-13:
                a, b, cur = a_new, b_new, sc_new
                stall = 0
                if it % 200 == 199:
                    b = _conv_full(a)
                    cur = 2.0 * n * float(b.max())
                if cur < best_sc - 1e-14:
                    best_sc = cur
                    best_a = a.copy()
            else:
                stall += 1
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 25:
                if stall % 2 == 0:
                    tau = max(80.0, tau * 0.55)
                else:
                    tau = min(tau_max, tau * 1.7)
            if stall > 160:
                a = _perturb(a, rng, max(1e-5, 0.35 / n))
                b = _conv_full(a)
                cur = 2.0 * n * float(b.max())
                tau = max(80.0, tau * 0.6)
                stall = 0

        it += 1

    if cur < best_sc - 1e-14:
        best_sc = cur
        best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a, best_sc


def _coord_polish(a0, stop, max_sweeps=600, seed=123):
    a = _clip_norm(a0)
    n = len(a)
    if n < 2:
        return a, _score_unnorm(a)
    b = _conv_full(a)
    cur = 2.0 * n * float(b.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = cur
    if not math.isfinite(best_sc):
        return best_a, best_sc

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a_max = float(a.max())

    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 6.0:
            break
        b = _conv_full(a)
        cur = 2.0 * n * float(b.max())
        pref, suff = _psmax(b)
        two_a = 2.0 * a
        a_max = float(a.max())
        improved = False
        order = rng.permutation(n)

        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > stop - 6.0:
                break

            old = float(a[i])
            x_base = max(4.0 / n, 2.0 * old + 2.0 / n, 2.0 / n, 0.35 * a_max + 1.0 / n)
            x_high = min(0.5, max(x_base, old + 1e-6))
            if x_high <= old + 1e-8:
                x_high = old + max(2.0 / n, 1e-5)

            K = 48
            xs = np.linspace(0.0, x_high, K)
            d = xs - old
            Snew = 1.0 + d
            valid = Snew > 1e-7
            if not np.any(valid):
                continue

            aff = b[i:i + n]
            left = i - 1
            right = i + n
            om = -np.inf
            if left >= 0:
                om = max(om, float(pref[left]))
            if right < b.size:
                om = max(om, float(suff[right]))

            Z = d[:, None] * two_a[None, :]
            if 0 <= i < n:
                Z[:, i] += d * d
            new_aff = aff[None, :] + Z
            maxb = np.maximum(new_aff.max(axis=1), om)
            sc = 2.0 * n * maxb / (Snew * Snew)
            sc[~valid] = np.inf
            sc[~np.isfinite(sc)] = np.inf

            idx = int(np.argmin(sc))
            bx = float(xs[idx])
            bs = float(sc[idx])
            if not math.isfinite(bs):
                continue

            if bs < cur - 1e-13:
                dw = x_high / max(1, K - 1)
                if 0 < idx < K - 1:
                    lo = max(0.0, bx - 3.0 * dw)
                    hi = min(x_high, bx + 3.0 * dw)
                    xf = np.linspace(lo, hi, 24)
                    df = xf - old
                    Sf = 1.0 + df
                    vf = Sf > 1e-7
                    Zf = df[:, None] * two_a[None, :]
                    if 0 <= i < n:
                        Zf[:, i] += df * df
                    maff = (aff[None, :] + Zf).max(axis=1)
                    mbf = np.maximum(maff, om)
                    scf = 2.0 * n * mbf / (Sf * Sf)
                    scf[~vf] = np.inf
                    scf[~np.isfinite(scf)] = np.inf
                    j = int(np.argmin(scf))
                    if math.isfinite(scf[j]) and scf[j] < bs - 1e-15:
                        bx = float(xf[j])
                        bs = float(scf[j])

            if math.isfinite(bs) and bs < cur - 1e-13:
                delta = bx - old
                if delta != 0.0:
                    b[i:i + n] += delta * two_a
                    b[2 * i] += delta * delta

                a[i] = bx
                Snew = 1.0 + delta
                if abs(Snew - 1.0) > 1e-12:
                    a /= Snew
                    b /= (Snew * Snew)

                np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
                np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
                cur = 2.0 * n * float(b.max())
                if cur < best_sc - 1e-13:
                    best_sc = cur
                    best_a = a.copy()
                improved = True

        if not improved:
            b = _conv_full(a)
            cur = 2.0 * n * float(b.max())
            if cur < best_sc - 1e-13:
                best_sc = cur
                best_a = a.copy()
            break

    if cur < best_sc - 1e-14:
        best_sc = cur
        best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def _interp_to(init, L):
    init = np.asarray(init, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if L <= 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    if init.size == L:
        return init.copy()
    oldx = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, init.size)
    newx = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L)
    out = np.interp(newx, oldx, init)
    return _clip_norm(out, L)


def _structured_inits(n, parent):
    out = [_clip_norm(parent, n)]
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    candidates = []

    candidates.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for alpha in (-1.0, -0.6, -0.3, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
        candidates.append(1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * t - 1.0))
        candidates.append(1.0 + alpha * ((2.0 * t - 1.0) ** 2 - 1.0 / 3.0))

    candidates.append(1.0 + 0.55 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t) + 0.25 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t + 0.55))
    candidates.append(1.0 + 0.55 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t - 0.9) + 0.25 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t))
    candidates.append((1.0 - t) ** 2 + 1e-3)
    candidates.append(t ** 2 + 1e-3)
    candidates.append(1.0 + 0.7 * np.maximum(0.0, (t - 0.65)) ** 2)
    candidates.append(1.0 + 0.7 * np.maximum(0.0, (0.35 - t)) ** 2)

    for f in candidates:
        f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
        f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
        f = np.clip(f, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if f.size != n:
            f = _interp_to(f, n)
        if float(f.sum()) > 0.01:
            out.append(_clip_norm(f, n))
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_stop = t0 + budget - 12.0

    parent = _get_parent()
    n_parent = max(2, len(parent))
    fallback = _clip_norm(parent, n_parent)

    best_seq = fallback.copy()
    best_score = _score_unnorm(best_seq)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        fallback = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0
        best_seq = fallback.copy()
        best_score = _score_unnorm(best_seq)

    def update_best(seq):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_score
        if seq is None:
            return
        seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
        seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if seq.size == 0 or float(seq.sum()) < 0.01:
            return
        sc = _score_unnorm(seq)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = seq.copy()

    inits = _structured_inits(n_parent, fallback)
    init_best = best_seq.copy()
    init_best_sc = best_score
    for seq in inits:
        sc = _score_unnorm(seq)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < init_best_sc:
            init_best = seq.copy()
            init_best_sc = sc

    if math.isfinite(init_best_sc) and init_best_sc < best_score - 1e-15:
        best_score = init_best_sc
        best_seq = init_best.copy()

    # Primary LP phase.
    lp_stop = min(hard_stop - 560.0, t0 + max(70.0, budget * 0.15))
    if time.monotonic() < lp_stop - 6.0:
        try:
            seq, sc = _lp_optimize(init_best, lp_stop, seed + 101)
        except Exception:
            seq, sc = init_best, _score_unnorm(init_best)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = _clip_norm(seq, len(seq))
            update_best(best_seq)

    # Primary gradient phase.
    grad_stop = min(hard_stop - 260.0, t0 + budget * 0.65)
    if time.monotonic() < grad_stop - 6.0:
        try:
            seq, sc = _grad_optimize(best_seq, grad_stop, seed + 202)
        except Exception:
            seq, sc = best_seq, best_score
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = _clip_norm(seq, len(seq))
            update_best(best_seq)

    # Coordinate polish.
    coord_stop = min(hard_stop - 130.0, t0 + budget * 0.90)
    if time.monotonic() < coord_stop - 8.0:
        try:
            seq, sc = _coord_polish(best_seq, coord_stop, max_sweeps=650, seed=303)
        except Exception:
            seq, sc = best_seq, best_score
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = _clip_norm(seq, len(seq))
            update_best(best_seq)

    # Try nearby lengths while beneficial.
    if best_score > _TARGET and time.monotonic() < hard_stop - 90.0:
        try_lengths = []
        for L in (n_parent - 16, n_parent - 8, n_parent + 8, n_parent + 16, 384, 416):
            if L >= 48 and L <= 1024 and L not in try_lengths:
                try_lengths.append(L)
        try_lengths.sort(key=lambda x: abs(x - len(best_seq)))

        remaining = hard_stop - 90.0 - time.monotonic()
        n_try = max(1, len(try_lengths[:3]))
        time_per = max(25.0, remaining / float(n_try))
        for L in try_lengths[:3]:
            if time.monotonic() >= hard_stop - 90.0:
                break
            if best_score <= _TARGET:
                break
            stop = min(hard_stop - 90.0, time.monotonic() + time_per)
            init = _interp_to(best_seq, L)
            try:
                seq, sc = _grad_optimize(init, stop, seed + 1000 + L)
            except Exception:
                seq, sc = init, _score_unnorm(init)
            if math.isfinite(sc):
                update_best(seq)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_seq = _clip_norm(seq, len(seq))

    # One more stress/perturbation gradient run if budget allows.
    if best_score > _TARGET and time.monotonic() < hard_stop - 70.0:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 999999)
        pert = _perturb(best_seq, rng, max(1e-4, 0.5 / max(1, len(best_seq))))
        alloc = max(40.0, (hard_stop - 70.0 - time.monotonic()) * 0.75)
        stop = min(hard_stop - 70.0, time.monotonic() + alloc)
        try:
            seq, sc = _grad_optimize(pert, stop, seed + 777)
        except Exception:
            seq, sc = pert, _score_unnorm(pert)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            update_best(seq)

    # Final coordinate polish.
    final_stop = hard_stop - 10.0
    if time.monotonic() < final_stop - 6.0:
        try:
            seq, sc = _coord_polish(best_seq, final_stop, max_sweeps=250, seed=424242)
        except Exception:
            seq, sc = best_seq, best_score
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = _clip_norm(seq, len(seq))
            update_best(best_seq)

    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_seq = np.where(np.isfinite(best_seq), best_seq, 0.0)
    best_seq = np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if best_seq.size == 0 or float(best_seq.sum()) < 0.01:
        best_seq = fallback.copy()

    def exact_score(seq):
        try:
            v = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in seq])
            return float(v)
        except Exception:
            return math.inf

    final_exact = exact_score(best_seq)
    if not math.isfinite(final_exact):
        fb_exact = exact_score(fallback)
        if math.isfinite(fb_exact):
            return [float(x) for x in fallback]
        return [1.0]

    fb_exact = exact_score(fallback)
    if math.isfinite(fb_exact) and fb_exact < final_exact - 1e-14:
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]

    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
